## §0 THESIS

Together the eight buy ONE thing the estate does not have today: a product whose every generated fact is either SHOWN to a customer or DECLARED dark by a typed, live-convicted row; whose every tunable is REGISTERED under an id, a unit and a draft/signed state the owner's pen flips in one sitting; whose every band is EXERCISED by the frozen corpus or exempted for a cited reason that retires itself; whose long-horizon worlds are held to a LIVENESS floor in every gate and to a 300-year PLATEAU in the register the tuning sitting signs against; whose customer documents are READ against a fixed rubric with checkable citations before anyone calls them legible; whose carrying-capacity term — already built and dark — gains the one legible sentence, the reading, the instruments and the sign-then-light structure that let it be lit honestly; whose custom text can enter only if the two paid renderers can DRAW it; and whose generation leaves the main thread on ONE code path proven byte-identical across main, worker and headless. All of it lands DARK and output-neutral before the GOLDEN freeze (the instruments at any slot; the machinery dark behind typed doors — HORIZON-DARK, a BIT claim), ⟦CHAIR-R9⟧ is LIT in the LIGHTING WAVE before the freeze as a DECLARED SHIFT by the owner's word ("oh light everything up before the exhaustive review.", 2026-09-02, §881.4 — so the freeze records the LIT world and the review reads the world the customer gets), has its tuning VALUES SIGNED LAST at the tuning sitting through the GOLDEN door with the owner's signature (the door signs values, not lightings), and spends **0 B** of the engine margin (661 B at T13's `594c7521f`, ⟦A47 O16⟧) by construction — every byte says its chunk, every prediction is measured, and every car STOPs on one byte rather than asking for a raise.

The one-sentence promise each keeps:

| mechanism | the promise it keeps |
|---|---|
| **CAPACITY** | The built two-sided rate model behind `demographicsEnabled` IS the capacity term; CAPACITY adds only the sign-then-light surface, THE CROWDING LINE ("growth slowed because the fields are full", addressed), THE READING (a lazy dossier leaf, null when dark), the 30-year envelope pin plus four receipt detectors, and the owner's ONE lighting act — nothing writes the head count twice. |
| **TUNEREG** | Every `_TUNING` table and every declared tuning home is inventoried by a collision-proof `file#export` id with measured leaves and dependents, declared with a unit from a closed vocabulary and a `draft \| signed@vN` state, held by shrink-only ratchets, and cannot move once signed except as version N+1 through a signing record the GOLDEN door's shift record names — zero product bytes, values stay in their homes. |
| **SOAKCHAIN** | One pure invariants module gives the watchdog a liveness floor, the tripwire registry a caller and a `liveness_floor` row, the check chain a 100-year in-gate century leg at the product grain, and the tuning sitting a scheduled, register-frozen 300-year LIT curve — one soak, flag-varied, zero `src/` bytes. |
| **READERREVIEW** | The exhaustive review is a reader protocol over a deterministic corpus of 30-year campaigns rendered headlessly into every customer document, read by Fable seats against a fixed rubric whose every answer cites a resolvable address chain, hunted by an adversary over a closed contradiction vocabulary, refuted before believed, and discharged into a typed fix backlog the validator refuses to let become an engine car. |
| **COVERAGE** | The BAND CENSUS counts settlements carrying each band of each banded field over the same 525 rows the golden regenerates, refuses any band under N without a self-retiring typed exemption, freezes the counts bound to the manifest's byte-sha, and proposes widenings as door-shaped `enroll` rows the owner signs at the freeze. |
| **WRWALKER** | The dual of the OSR — over the same executed `corpus.shapes` — asks of every written key whether a customer-facing renderer root can reach a read of it, grades the answer R/N, and reds at ceiling 0 any DARK key that is neither a typed, live-convicted DARK-register row nor a banked shrink-only cohort member; it hands CHARSET the surface-class reach map and READERREVIEW the pending-surface backlog. |
| **CHARSET** | A zero-import lazy leaf checks every free-text custom-content codepoint against the ALLOWED SET OF THE SURFACES THAT FIELD REACHES, each set DERIVED by the manifest compiler from the renderers' own bytes (8 embedded faces ∩ = 759; jsPDF WinAnsi 218; the executed sanitiser 191), rejects with six typed codes and a fix hint, ships behind `enforcement: report`, never refuses content that already exists, and lands zero eager bytes. |
| **WORKER** | The generation LANE leaves the eager slice for a lazy module with one transport-neutral core that the Web Worker shell and the in-thread fallback both call over cloned inputs; the protocol is typed and closed; byte identity is proven in-thread ≡ Worker-double ≡ Node isolate over a tier×culture sample and golden 0/525, and the BROWSER Worker leg is pinned through the estate's existing Playwright harness before the flag lights (⟦A11 P6⟧⟦CHAIR-R8⟧); it ships DARK behind `generationWorker: false`, and its first act is to MEASURE whether the extraction shrinks first paint. |

### §1.5 THE TUNING REGISTER AND ITS SIGNATURE VERSION (TUNEREG · CAPACITY · READERREVIEW · SOAKCHAIN · GOLDEN)

- **`tuningIdFor(file, exportName) → '<repo-relative file>#<exportName>'`**; leaf path `'<id>.<key>[.<sub>]'` (`scripts/lib/tuning-inventory.mjs`). Never the bare export name — three names collide (`REACTION_TUNING` ×3, `LADDER_TUNING` ×2, `DISPATCH_TUNING` ×2, CONFIRMED).
- **THE MEASURED INVENTORY** `tests/lint/.tuning-inventory.json` (machine-written by the refreeze ritual ONLY; `TuningInventory` typedef §3.2) and **THE DECLARED REGISTER** `tests/lint/.tuning-register.json` (the pen's file; `TuningRegister` typedef §3.2: `registerVersion`, `signatureVersion`, `signatures[]`, `unitVocabulary` (14 closed words), `homes[]`, `tables{}`, `roles{}`, `soakBandAliases{}`).
- **`signatureStateOf(register, id) → { status: 'draft'|'signed', signedAt: number|null, version: number }`** and **`tuningRegisterFingerprint(root) → { signatureVersion, measuredAtSha, declaredSha256, inventorySha256 }`** — the two hooks every consumer reads. READERREVIEW's PLAUSIBLE `tuningRegisterState()` is RETIRED: its manifest `tuning` block `{ state: 'unregistered'|'draft'|'signed', version, signedBy, draftIds }` is COMPOSED from `tuningRegisterFingerprint` (version) + `signatureStateOf` over every id a reader cites + `signatures[N].signedBy`; `state: 'unregistered'` (every value DRAFT) when the two files are absent — fail-closed.
- **THE SIGNATURE VERSION** is TUNEREG's integer `version` (dense from 1) with `date`, `signedBy`, `odqRow`, `ownerWords`, `ids[]`, `digests{}`, `goldenShiftRecord`. ⟦A9 P4⟧⟦CHAIR-R7⟧ The version GATES the door only through TUNEREG's own arms 33 / 33b / 33c (signature ⇒ record AND record ⇒ signature, both text-read from `.golden-freeze-register.json` and `docs/shift-records/`), because the door itself verifies form only; X-1 asks GOLDEN's lane to ACCEPT `tuningVersion` as a REQUIRED field on any record whose `cause` matches the RE, so the door refuses the mismatch at the write — offered, never assumed. CAPACITY's `DEMOGRAPHIC_TUNING_PROVENANCE.{signedBy, odqRow, signedOn}` are the SAME values copied from `signatures[N]` (`signedOn = date`) and arm 34 pins them equal — CAPACITY's "signature version = `{odqRow, signedOn}`" is a projection of TUNEREG's version, not a second version.
- **THE SIGNING RECORD** `docs/tuning-signatures/<date>-v<N>.json` `{ ownerWords, ownerDate, odqRow, seat, version, ids[], note }` (a NEW dir; NOT `docs/shift-records/`, which GOLDEN's walker forbids before the freeze — `goldenFreeze.walker.test.js:889-891`, CONFIRMED at `ba08939d7`).
- **`TUNING_SHIFT_CAUSE_RE = /^tuning-register v(\d+) signed (§\S+)/`** — the GOLDEN shift record's `cause` for the version's re-record (only if the retune moved a golden; see §1.9 for the two-record law).
- **`homes[]` roster** (kind `'tables'` | `'signature-record'`): the unsuffixed tuning exports the `_TUNING` glob cannot see — `densityBands.js` ×10, `bandedStock.js#HALF_LIFE_WEEKS`, `intervalWeeks.js#INTERVAL_WEEKS`, `faithChannelBindings.js#CAUSAL_SWING`, post-TAIL-F `faithTuningSurface.js` ×3, and **the demographics family EXTENDED to CAPACITY's 16-row `DEMOGRAPHIC_TUNING_COVERAGE`** (§13 resolves the 7-vs-16 disagreement): the roster is TEXT-READ from the coverage span in `demographicsRates.js` by the same brace-matching span machine (the walker imports NO src module — TUNEREG's law stands), with an arm "the demographics homes equal the coverage roster's tunable ids, both ways". Signature-record homes declare `words: ['signed','live'] | ['signed']` so CAPACITY's one-word record and D4/F6c's two-word records are one arm.
- **THE `lit ⇒ declared` ARM** (⟦CHAIR-R9⟧ re-cut from `lit ⇒ signed`; CAPACITY's requirement, TUNEREG's walker): the owner's word lights the term in the LIGHTING WAVE, BEFORE the values are signed, so 'a lit preset ⇒ a signed surface' would red the lighting itself; the arm now reads — for every shipped preset in `SIMULATION_RULE_PRESETS`, a `demographicsEnabled: true` ⇒ `DEMOGRAPHIC_TUNING_SIGNATURE.lit` is a LIGHTING RECORD `{ odqRow: /§\d+/, litOn: <date>, presets: [every lit preset, exact] }` (the wave's declaration, written in the SAME commit as the flips), and — post-sitting — `signed === true` ⇒ `signatures[N].ids` covers the demographics family (arm 43 binds the receipt); a lit preset with NO lighting record reds (the plant: a flipped scratch preset without the record); ⟦A24 E8⟧ BOTH reads go through `codeOnly(src)` — `simulationRules.js:849` already carries a docblock naming the flag and CAPACITY's own `PROVENANCE.ritual` string spells the flip in PROSE (the §880.7 class; the config-key walkers convicted a docblock and were cured by `tests/helpers/codeOnlySource.js`) — with two negative controls (a docblock containing `demographicsEnabled: true` in a scratch preset copy must NOT satisfy; a docblock containing `lit:` in a scratch rates copy must NOT satisfy) beside the planted-preset positive control — arm 42, landed by CAPACITY C1 as a rider on `tests/lint/tuningRegister.walker.test.js` (TUNEREG must be in the tree first — §10: HORIZON-INSTR precedes HORIZON-DARK).

Every row `{ id, class, band, home (≥ 40 chars, its derivation), detect(receipt) → string[] }`, reads no clock, returns `[]` on `{}`/`null`.
- **THE CALLER** `scripts/soak/evaluate.mjs` `evaluateReceipt(receipt, { profile, rolling, restored, behavioralPropertiesPassing }) → { findings, observability, deterministicFirings, annotated }` — writes the `deterministicFirings`/`fullInstrument`/`provisional`/`rolling`/`restored` annotation SK-5's `freezeBlockers` reads (`curveBands.mjs:66-80`); CLI `scripts/soak/evaluate-receipt.mjs` (exit 0/1/2) as a step on the weekly `world-soak` job and the research job; the pool runner `run.mjs` hands `findings` to `rollingReport` and `firings` to the seed ledger.
- **THE REGISTER** `tests/soak-harness/.soak-register.json` — `registerVersion 1`, `figureDirections = ['shrink','floor','band','exact','ceiling','report']`, `cells{ '<profile>/<caseId>': { frozenAtSha, measuredBy, date, note, identity: { seed, years, settlements, lighting, receiptSchemaVersion, settlementIds }, figures{ 'realm.runawayCount' shrink · 'realm.flooredCount' shrink · 'realm.unlawfulZeroCount' shrink · 'realm.bifurcated' shrink · 'realm.ratio' band 0.25 · 'population.<id>.shape' exact · 'population.<id>.final' band 0.10 · 'liveness.minDistinctTypesPerDecade' floor · 'liveness.minEventsPerSettlementDecade' floor · 'liveness.minHashMovesPerDecade' floor · 'liveness.silentDecades' shrink · 'liveness.frozenDecades' shrink · 'bytes.maxRealmBytes' band 0.10 · 'bytes.realmBytesAtYear.NNN' band 0.10 · 'cost.peakHeapUsedBytes' ceiling 838860800 host-observability · 'cost.primaryMs' report } } }`. Two genesis cells: `research-lit-4s/research-lit-4s-300y-4s-seed1` (the interim cell that fits a hosted 6-h job) and `research-lit/research-lit-300y-12s-seed1` (THE cell the sitting signs against). `scripts/soak/register.mjs`: `SETTLEMENT_SHAPES = ['plateau','runaway','floored','died','other']`, `settlementShapeOf(series)`, `deriveRegisterFigures(annotatedReceipt)`, `compareRegister(committed, measured) → RegisterFinding[]` (`kind ∈ grew | fell_below_floor | outside_band | shape_changed | over_ceiling | missing`), `applyRefreeze`; CLI `scripts/soak/soak-register.mjs --compare | --write` (`SOAK_REGISTER_REFREEZE`, `SOAK_REGISTER_NOTE`, `SOAK_REGISTER_DECLARED`; dirty-tree refusal; atomic; EXITS NON-ZERO BY DESIGN after a write). ⟦A22 E6⟧⟦CHAIR-R6⟧ **The growth door, restored and governed** — `--write` REFUSES raising a `shrink` figure or lowering a `floor` figure even when declared (the SOAKCHAIN design's own `SOAK_REGISTER_ALLOW_DEBT=<figure>` clause, which the integrator's fold listed in the CLI parenthesis and defined nowhere) unless the write carries `--charter=§NNN` AND `SOAK_REGISTER_NOTE` ≥ 60 chars, appends `debtHistory[] { figure, from, to, charter, seat, date }` to the register, and the set of figures ever banked upward is a shrink-only population the plain `--compare` prints as a HOLD line — an env var + a free-text note (the weakest of the estate's four growth doors beside TUNEREG's `DECLARED_GROWTH`, WRWALKER's `--rebank --charter` and GOLDEN's signed record) would have let a runaway be banked by note, the exact class the register exists to refuse; SOAK-4's 'accept the honest red' is the ONLY sanctioned use before the sitting.
- **CAPACITY's 300-year evidence** is the `research-lit/…` cell (its `frozenAtSha` + `identity.lighting` is the citation the tuning signature record carries); the receipt gains `tuningRegister: TuningRegisterFingerprint` (§1.5) so a soak proves the register version it ran under. ⟦A43 O10⟧ The arc runs the terminal soak BEFORE the tuning signature, so that receipt is measured under `signatureVersion 0` (draft values); if the sitting retunes ANY demographics id (TR-5's drift-suspected families, CAP-3's recalibration, CAP-7(b)), the signed tree is NOT the plateaued tree — TUNEREG arm 43 refuses `signatures[N]` over a demographics id whose cited cell's `tuningRegister.declaredSha256` does not cover that id's digest at N, and owner row CAP-10 rules the consequence in one word: RE-RUN the 12 s cell after the retune (≈ 17.6 h, before push) or sign those ids UNCHANGED and defer their retunes to a post-launch signed version. 'Record C' is never left to IMPLY the re-run. **The DARK twin** (the 300y×12s `research` profile run under the soak's own overlay `--lighting demographicsEnabled=false` — ⟦CHAIR-R9⟧ after the wave the shipped preset is LIT, so 'the shipped profile' no longer re-creates the runaway by itself — re-creating the runaway as a fresh receipt; CAPACITY's negative control; the original 2.6 MB receipt is in git on no line) is a ONE-TIME manual run at the terminal soak, cited in the ODQ row and the shift record's evidence, NOT a register cell (§13 C7: a cell designed to fail `population_bounded` cannot mint — `--write` refuses `passed !== true`).

} } }] }`; `--verify <manifest>` re-renders and exits non-zero on the first differing hash — the corpus's determinism bit.

### §1.9 THE GOLDEN DOOR AND THE TWO-RECORD LAW OF THE SITTING (GOLDEN as built · TUNEREG · CAPACITY · COVERAGE · READERREVIEW)

### §1.12 THE WORKER PROTOCOL AND THE ONE CORE (WORKER · SOAKCHAIN · COVERAGE · READERREVIEW · WRWALKER)

- **`src/lib/generationProtocol.js`** (ZERO-IMPORT leaf): `GENERATION_WORKER_CONTRACT = 'settlementforge:generation-worker:v1'`, `GENERATION_PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1`, `GENERATION_OPS = ['settlement']` (Car 2 `+ 'instant-world'`; Car 3 `+ 'regen-npcs','regen-history'`), `TRANSPORT_OUTCOMES = { WORKER: 'worker', FLAG_OFF: 'in-thread:flag-off', NO_WORKER: 'in-thread:no-worker', CONSTRUCT_FAILED: 'in-thread:construct-failed', TRANSPORT_FAILED: 'in-thread:transport-failed' }`, `GENERATION_ERROR_CODES = ['pipeline_threw','protocol_mismatch','request_invalid','cancelled']`; typedefs `GenerationSettlementPayload { fullConfig, importedNeighbour, seed (minted on MAIN), contentRuntime { customContent, contentTunables, explicitConfigFields, contentProvenance }, previousSettlement, locks }`, `GenerationRequest { kind: 'generation.request', v: 1, op, requestId ('generation-${++sequence}', never a clock), payload }`, `GenerationStepEvent`, `GenerationSettlementResult { settlement, preservation, pipelineHistory[{ id, summary }], resolvedConfig }`, `GenerationResultPacket`, `GenerationErrorPacket`. ## §2 CAPACITY — carrying capacity as a MODEL CHANGE that is already built and dark

### §2.0 Thesis
The term the brief describes is BUILT and DARK at `60255ca8e`: THE DEMOGRAPHIC ENGINE (wave P, landed 2026-08-01) behind `demographicsEnabled` — births suppressed and deaths raised against `bound = min(K_food, D_tier)` DERIVED at pulse time from receipted stocks, with a FIXED POINT at 76–83 % of the bound and "nothing in this file clamps a population" (CONFIRMED `demographicsKernel.js:5-14, :286-313`; `demographicsRates.js:559-597`). The brief's literal `effective growth = base × (1 − pop/capacity)` is a DIFFERENT functional form that would be a SECOND WRITER on the same head count; CAPACITY REFUSES it and architects the ESTATE around the built term: (a) ⟦CHAIR-R9⟧ a light-DECLARED-then-sign structure (the sign-then-light order the design built is inverted by the owner's word: the term LIGHTS in the lighting wave in every preset, its values are SIGNED last); (b) THE CROWDING LINE; (c) THE READING; (d) THE INSTRUMENTS; (e) THE LIGHTING CAR (C4′, the wave's) and THE SIGNATURE (C5, the sitting's). Every product byte lands DARK-inert in the lazy pulse chunk (0 B predicted on both ceilings; MEASURED before HOLD); the TUNING-DESK's item 1 is corrected from "choose the cure" to "sign the bands of the cure that landed and LIT".

### §2.1 The mechanism
**The term (CONFIRMED).** Per settlement, per weekly tick, under `demographicsEnabled === true`: `food = foodCapacityOf(...)` (K_food in MOUTHS: `dailyProduction × MOUTHS_PER_FOOD_UNIT[tier]` + `dailyNeed × importDependency × ARTERY_IMPORT_FACTORS[min(arteries,3)] × importFactorOf(works.imports) × mouthsPerUnit` − `local × OBLIGATION_DRAW_FRACTIONS[min(creditors,3)]`); `bound = effectiveBoundOf(food, densityCeilingOf(...))` (`min(K_food, D_tier)`, `binding: 'granary'|'walls'`, `foodKnown`); `pressure01 = clamp(pop / max(1, bound), 0, PRESSURE_MAX 2)` (the READ is clamped, the population never — J-P5); `crowding = clamp01((pressure01 − BIRTH_EASE 0.75) / 0.25)`; `birth01 = BIRTH_BANDS[tier] × (1 − BIRTH_SUPPRESSION_MAX 0.90 × crowding)`; `strain = clamp(pressure01 − DEATH_EASE 0.70, 0, DEATH_STRAIN_CAP 1.30)`; `death01 = NATURAL_DEATH_BANDS[tier] × max(1, 1 + 2.0·strain + 3.0·deficit01 + 3.0·crisis01·occupancy)`; `after = max(min(named, before), before + births − deaths)` (`demographicsKernel.js:313` — the H3 named-cast floor, NO clamp). Fixed point `p* = (3.7 + 0.4r)/(3.6 + 2r), r = d0/b0` → 0.83 (thorp) … 0.76 (metropolis). Pinned three ways: `tests/domain/demographicsCure.test.js:108-149` (300 y × 52 ticks, six-settlement realm; y300/bound ≤ 1.05; second half flat ≤ 5 %; century multipliers < 50; two executed REVERTs + RESTORE), `demographicsFloor.test.js` (the floored-six unfreeze), the certification invariant `the_bound_is_a_plateau_and_not_a_clamp` (`subsystemRowsGrowth.js`).

> **JUDGMENT (CAPACITY): chose the built two-sided rate model (`demographicsEnabled`) as THE capacity term over the brief's literal logistic damper because (i) the literal is a second writer on the same head count, forbidden by design §0b and law 1 ("no growth term that is not a birth, no shrink that is not a death", `DESIGN_DEMOGRAPHIC_ENGINE.md:153-156`); (ii) the built form already satisfies every proof clause the brief names with executed pins; (iii) a logistic on growth alone leaves the death side to the legacy lane, re-forking the reconciliation P4 made. Recorded as a divergence from the brief's wording — vetoable; the veto builds the logistic as a THIRD rate term inside `demographicRates()` (never a clamp), and every §2.5 pin re-derives.**

**The derivation (which receipted stocks feed capacity today, CONFIRMED):** arable/food = K_food's LOCAL side (`foodLedger.dailyProduction`, generation-frozen); trade reach = K_food's IMPORT side (arteries from `interdictableArteries`); standing obligations = K_food's debit (`grain_relief` records); housing = D_tier (`DENSITY_CEILINGS[tier] × TERRAIN_DENSITY_ADJUST[terrain] × infrastructureFactorOf(works.infrastructure)`); **walls/defense NOT an input** (`defenseLedger` is a push driver and raid-exposure input; the engine's word `walls` means the DENSITY ceiling); **water — no receipted stock exists** (`settlement.schema.js` has no water field); reserves DELIBERATELY NOT ("a warehouse of grain buys TIME", design §2b). ⚠ THE UNIT LAW (§711.6): K_food is in MOUTHS; `deficitPct` is a percent of a GENERATION-FROZEN need and "TWICE unusable as an arbiter" — no new reader touches it.

> **JUDGMENT (CAPACITY): chose NOT to add defense/walls as a capacity input over adding `defenseLedger.readinessScore` as a D_tier multiplier, because a new input moves the fixed point of every settlement (a MODEL change the tuning pass would have to re-sign from scratch), a readiness score is a security posture not a housing bound (FINITE SEMANTICS: a stock must mean what it feeds), and defense already couples to demography where it belongs (raid exposure; the DEFENSE push driver). Vetoable; the veto is OWNER ROW CAP-4.** A water stock is GENUINELY NEW CAPABILITY (a generator field, a persisted shape, a new writer) — owner row CAP-5, not designed.

**The bands — no new ladder minted.** The displayable capacity state is the COMPOSITION of existing closed ladders, typed as **`DemographicReadingView`** `{ occupancy ∈ OVERFLOW_BANDS ['easy','filling','pressed','overflowing'], binding ∈ BINDING_KINDS, foodKnown, foodFlow ∈ FOOD_FLOW_BANDS, reserve ∈ RESERVE_BANDS, urbanLoad ∈ URBAN_LOAD_BANDS, births ∈ BIRTH_BAND_WORDS, deaths ∈ DEATH_BAND_WORDS, sentence (authored, no digit/ratio/band token/id) }`. "at capacity" = `occupancy ∈ {pressed, overflowing}`; "crowded" = `urbanLoad ∈ {packed, overspilled}`. ⟦A59 L10⟧ The Room to Grow section's FOUR chips are NAMED — occupancy · binding · foodFlow · urbanLoad — and each renders through an authored `CHIP_LABELS` map inside the leaf ('bound by its granaries', 'pressed for room', 'food short', 'packed quarters'), pinned by the SAME 'no band token reaches the surface' test the sentence gets: a chip reading `walls` or `pressed` beside the sentence is a label without its noun (Q-CAP-2's own 'a band word without a cause', with the cause one line away); the `proseLeak` walker's fixture render includes the Outlook tab's Room to Grow markup. Design §2b's "THREE READINGS, NEVER ONE DIAGNOSIS" is honoured; `spBandFamilies.walker`'s disjointness law untouched; `pressure01` stays DERIVED, never persisted — nothing owner-gated.

**THE CROWDING LINE (NEW).** The gap (CONFIRMED): the Herald emits only THE HUNGER LINE and THE DEPARTURE LINE (`demographicsHerald.js:167-263`); the kernel's own sentence ("The granaries are the wall at N mouths…") rides a receipt `applyPulseMover` DROPS (`region/wizardNews.js:978-990`; `demographicsObservation.js:17-19`). At equilibrium EVERY settled settlement has suppressed births forever (fixed point 0.76–0.83 vs `BIRTH_EASE` 0.75), so a STATE-keyed line is wallpaper; the Herald's law is "emitted only when the reading crosses an authored floor, at most one of each kind per settlement per tick" (`:40-43`). The line fires on a CROSSING of the OVERFLOW ladder computed from the step receipt's own `before`/`after` against `bound` — no persisted prior state, no new key: `FILLED iff rankBefore < rank('filling') <= rankAfter`; `THINNED iff rankBefore == rank('overflowing') && rankAfter <= rank('pressed')`. Floors (HERALD_TUNING, draft, owner-signed): `CROWDING_LINE_MIN_SOULS: 60` (below the thorp ceiling a one-soul jitter re-crosses); hunger takes precedence (one cause per settlement per tick). Envelope (`demographicNews()` reused verbatim): `impactKind: 'population_crowding'`, `causeClass: 'crowding'`, `slug: 'crowding'`, `ids/names`, `scope: 'local'`, `significance: 'notable'`, severity 0.35 (filled) / 0.50 (thinned), score 48/56, `tags: ['world_pulse','demographics','crowding']`, `sourceEventId: demographics.crowding.<id>.<tick>`. **Prose table (direction × binding):** filled·granary `"${name} has grown as large as its fields will feed"` / summary "…has filled what its fields can feed. Fewer children are born there now, and the place will hold rather than grow." (⟦A55 L5⟧ two sentences) / reasons `"The granaries of ${name} are the wall, and the harvest reaches it."`, `"Growth slowed because the fields are full."` (the brief's sentence, verbatim `reasons[1]`); filled·walls "…as large as its walls will hold" / ⟦A55 L5⟧ "There is no ground left inside the walls of ${name} to build on." / "Growth slowed because there is no room left to build."; filled·food-unknown "…as large as its ground will hold" / "The ground itself has no more room at ${name}." / "Growth slowed because the place is full."; thinned·granary "…has thinned to what its fields will feed" / "The granaries of ${name} are the wall, and the place had grown past it." / "The lean years pressed until the count fit the harvest."; thinned·walls "…has thinned to what its walls will hold" / "${name} had spilled past its walls, and the crowding thinned it." / "Packed quarters pressed until the count fit the ground."; thinned·food-unknown follows walls with "the ground itself". ⟦A55 L5⟧ The two walls cells and the food-unknown cell are REWRITTEN without the metaphor: the engine's word `walls` means the DENSITY ceiling, and 'the walls of ${name} are the wall' reads to a regular human as a typo, not a translation (the game-grade law: TRANSLATE the formula, never rename it — the shipped hunger line already avoids the idiom outside the granary case, `demographicsHerald.js:195-197`); every SUMMARY cell is split into TWO sentences (the bible's 'one idea per sentence'); 'Growth slowed because the fields are full.' stays verbatim; the six cells join the news-headline/voice contract so `voiceMechanics` (⟦A17 E1⟧) and a 'no noun is its own predicate' pin hold them. A NEW `impactKind` is minted (no existing kind means "growth bounded by the place's capacity"); the three registrations are the cost; the token carries `population` so the soak's mover-family classifier files it under `population` (§2.7 R6).

> **JUDGMENT (CAPACITY): chose a CROSSING-keyed line over the step receipt (both directions toward the fixed point) over (a) riding the P3 plan receipts (`demographic_plan_opened/closed` fire only at `pressed` 0.88 — ABOVE the fixed point, so a settlement that simply plateaus would never be told why) and (b) a state-keyed line (wallpaper at equilibrium). Vetoable; the cheapest veto is "filled only".**
> **JUDGMENT (CAPACITY): chose to MINT `population_crowding` over riding `urban_fabric` because a typed cause must mean what it says and `urban_fabric` already has one producer whose routing and voice were decided for its own nature. Vetoable; the veto rides `urban_fabric` and pays no registration.**
> **JUDGMENT (integrator): the crowding severities/scores (0.35 / 0.50 / 48 / 56) and every new numeric literal of the line live INSIDE `HERALD_TUNING` (a P1 span), never as bare decimals in `demographicsHerald.js` code — TUNEREG's P3 arm "no file exceeds its bare-decimal baseline" would otherwise red C1 (§13 C5). — vetoable.**

**The door — unchanged (CONFIRMED).** ONE reader of the flag INSIDE the kernel (`demographicsActive`); dark ⇒ `advanceDemographics` returns the SAME references before composing any news, so the line and the reading are dark by construction. ⟦A12 P7⟧ But the FLAG has seven read sites across six worldPulse files (§1.4), so the lighting's blast radius is six lanes beside the kernel, not 'the crowding line + the reading + the rate term'; the lighting car's dry run enumerates the goldens the flip moves BY LANE and the ledger row states the full radius. Dormancy bits: FROZEN trace `2b2d754f4aed09fd`, the four `demographicsFloor.test.js` §8 hashes, the 525-row golden, plus each non-kernel site's own fence (§1.4).

> **JUDGMENT (CAPACITY): ONE door (`demographicsEnabled`) for the term, the line and the reading over a second `capacityNewsEnabled`/`capacityReadEnabled` flag — two lighting words for one mechanism are two notions of "lit" (P5b's "NEVER two notions of 'full'"), and a display of a dark mechanism must be honest ABSENCE. Vetoable; the veto adds a virtual `capacityLegibilityEnabled` and pays a new flag's lit-coverage and lighting-census bills.**

**The signature surface — sign, then light, structurally (NEW, appended to THE AUTHORED TABLES section of `demographicsRates.js`):** `DEMOGRAPHIC_TUNING_SIGNATURE = Object.freeze({ signed: false, lit: null })` (⟦CHAIR-R9⟧ `lit` becomes `{ odqRow, litOn, presets[] }` in the wave's commit; the LIVE word already exists — it is the preset flag); `DEMOGRAPHIC_TUNING_PROVENANCE = { status: 'CANDIDATE, OWNER-UNSIGNED (…)', signedBy: null, odqRow: null, signedOn: null, ritual: 'To light: the lighting wave flips demographicsEnabled in every shipped preset and writes lit {odqRow, litOn, presets} here in the same commit, as a declared shift. To sign: set signed true and write signedBy, odqRow and signedOn here in one diff at the tuning sitting. The register walker refuses a lit preset with no lighting record.' (⟦CHAIR-R9⟧ the order inverted by the owner's word; ⟦A17 E1⟧ written without an em dash or a `!`), coverage: '…' }`; `DEMOGRAPHIC_TUNING_COVERAGE` = 16 tunable rows by `file:export` id with `unit` (`demographicsRates.js:MOUTHS_PER_FOOD_UNIT` mouths/daily food unit · `DENSITY_CEILINGS` souls · `TERRAIN_DENSITY_ADJUST` multiplier · `BIRTH_BANDS` fraction/tick · `NATURAL_DEATH_BANDS` fraction/tick · `DEMOGRAPHIC_TUNING` (13 keys, mixed per key) · `CRISIS_MORTALITY_WEIGHTS` weight01 · `demographicsPushPull.js:PUSH_DRIVER_BANDS`, `:PUSH_PULL_TUNING` · `demographicsResponses.js:OVERFLOW_THRESHOLDS` pressure01, `:RESPONSE_WEIGHTS`, `:RESPONSE_TUNING` · `demographicsPlans.js:PLAN_TUNING` · `demographicsWorks.js:WORKS_TUNING` · `demographicsHerald.js:HERALD_TUNING` · `demographicsRisk.js:RISK_TUNING` · `demographicsWar.js:WAR_DEMOGRAPHIC_TUNING` · `demographicsLadder.js:LADDER_TUNING` (⚠ collides with `npcLadderState.js:LADDER_TUNING` — the `file:export` id is why) · `demographicsLand.js:SPATIAL_LAW_TUNING`, `:TIER_ELBOW`, `:MIN_SEPARATION_BANDS` · `migrationKernel.js:MIGRATION_KERNEL_TUNING.CONGEST_DECAY`) + 8 vocabulary rows by reference (`BIRTH_BAND_WORDS, DEATH_BAND_WORDS, BINDING_KINDS, IMPORT_SOURCES, FOOD_FLOW_BANDS, RESERVE_BANDS, URBAN_LOAD_BANDS, OVERFLOW_BANDS`); the exact key counts of the aggregators are the lane's read. The "lit ⇒ signed" law is a WALKER ARM over the preset (§1.5 arm 42).

> **JUDGMENT (CAPACITY): a THIN roster in `demographicsRates.js` (tables stay in their ten homes; 19 importers of that file alone) over F6c's relocation of every table into one surface file (10 src files + identity re-export pins) and over a NEW `demographicsTuningSurface.js` leaf (owes the new-leaf censuses for ~90 bytes of record). Vetoable; the veto is the F6c relocation.**

Hazards: an EAGER importer of the leaf re-parents the pulse family into first paint (FP-G17; the sentinel arm guards); a lazy tab statically importing a pulse module hoists shared modules (lazy → lazy is the safe direction; the lane MUST confirm the Outlook chunk does not statically import `engine-*.js` — cure = dynamic `import()` of the leaf).

## §3 TUNEREG — one signed tuning register before the tuning sitting

### §3.0 Thesis
An inventory-ratchet instrument plus a signature ledger over the tuning estate, landing ZERO product bytes: one MEASURED inventory of every tuning table by `file#export` id (keys, leaf values, span digest, measured dependents) joined to one DECLARED register carrying role, unit (14-word closed vocabulary), band and `draft | signed@vN`; a walker refusing an unregistered table, holding three shrink-only populations under monotone-down ceilings, flagging one-role-two-values divergences unless typed, cross-checking the soak-band manifest's `constantIds`, and pinning the D4/F6c/CAPACITY records to their rows; a refreeze ritual that cannot move a SIGNED digest without a signing record whose version the GOLDEN door's record A names as its ONE cause. Values stay in their homes (a REGISTER, not a rewrite); the only physical move is same-value duplicates folded by import (car 4). The register lands dark before the freeze; the sitting signs it; a signed version is one declared shift.

**Re-verified before building (CONFIRMED at `60255ca8e`):** 208 `const *_TUNING` definitions under `src/` (206 under `src/domain/`; 3 module-local; 208/208 `Object.freeze`; 1,870 top-level keys; 12 shorthand aggregators); the three colliding names; the duplicate families (`PRUNE_EPSILON` 0.02 vs 0.05 ×8; `INTERVAL_WEEKS` in 4 homes; `EXODUS_FLOOR` 0.04 vs 300; `SCORE_MAX` 12 ×3 vs 0.95; `MAX_LOOKBACK_TICKS` 260 ×3 / 312 / 24; 13 hand-keyed `Math.pow(0.5, …)` sites); `relationshipRulesCore.js` 302 decimal literals / 1 named const, `relationshipRulesAdversarial.js` 208 / 1; `src/domain/tuning/` has NO src importer; the walker idiom (`transcendentalMathBaseline.test.js` + `count-transcendental-math.mjs`), the `_doc`/refreeze idiom, `codeOnly`; D4 `{signed:false, live:false}` at `4e591b8e9` (in the COUPLED consist), F6c at `94e461bc4` (TAIL-F); the GOLDEN door as built (+ `goldenFreeze.walker.test.js:889-891`: no record file while UNFROZEN); `ENFORCER_DIRS` is EIGHT (`tests/generators` joined 2026-08-30) — the recon's "seven" corrected; `EACH_FAMILY_PARK_CEILING = 111` zero headroom; `pulseKernel.js` does not import `intervalWeeks.js` and `pulseKernelLineAddress.walker.test.js` exists (why car 4 leaves it).

### §3.1 The mechanism
**STATE YOUR SURFACE BEFORE YOUR ARMS.** Three populations, every measurement a text measurement over `codeOnly`-stripped source:
- **P1 — TABLES**: every `^\s*(export )?const [A-Za-z0-9_]*_TUNING\b` (208) PLUS every `(file, export)` in the declared `homes[]` (§1.5). Scope: all of `src/` + the roster. TOTALITY both ways, no baseline, no ceiling — a new table reds by id until registered.
- **P2 — NAMED NUMERIC CONSTS**: `^(export )?const [A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\s*=\s*-?\d+(\.\d+)?\s*;` at module top level, NOT inside a P1 span, NOT an aggregator leaf. Scope `src/domain`, `src/generators`. SHRINK-ONLY per file, new files at 0, exact tree match, monotone-down `UNREGISTERED_NAMED_CEILING`; growth only via `DECLARED_GROWTH`.
- **P3 — BARE DECIMAL LITERALS**: `(?<![\w.])\d+\.\d+(?![\w.])` in `codeOnly` text outside every P1 span and P2 line. Same scope; `BARE_DECIMAL_CEILING`. CANNOT-CATCH (stated, pinned by fixture): integer literals (`>= 30` ×5 in `capacityModel.js`, cooldowns 52/104/156, the 6-tick window ×5 — owner row TR-7), regex literals; a decimal in `${…}` IS counted; in a comment/string is NOT (the URL-trap plant proves the strip); `1.0`/`0.5` counted like any other.

> **JUDGMENT (TUNEREG): P2/P3 scope = `src/domain` + `src/generators` over "all of src" because those two trees are exactly the seeded pipeline (`autoTunableRegistry.js:46-48`'s golden-shifting set) and `src/data`'s catalogs carry thousands of non-tuning numerics — vetoable; the two `_TUNING` tables outside src/domain (`src/data/informationBrokerageTuning.js:107`, `src/lib/townScene/adaptiveQuality.js:32`) are still P1.**

**P1 measurement.** id `file#export` (module-local tables carry `exported: false`); the span = brace-matched text to the close of the outermost `Object.freeze({…})` (the recon's `tunereg-keycount.mjs` state machine, 208/208 closed, adopted verbatim as `tableSpan`); leaves flattened to `path → LeafValue` (`number` | `{ expr }` | `{ array }`); idioms `literal | aggregator (one hop to a same-file module const, which is REMOVED from P2) | computed (e.g. `CAUSAL_SWING` imported; the register REQUIRES a `homes[]` row for the source) | mixed`; **the span digest** = `sha256(normalizeWhitespace(codeOnly(spanText)))` — blind to comments/reflow/docblocks, sees any value/key/structure change; `line` recorded, NEVER asserted (the `wizardNewsAuthoring` line-address hazard); **dependents MEASURED** (importers whose specifier resolves to the home file; re-exports followed one hop) — the ESTATE's 113/206 zero-consumer tables become a measured column the review reads.

**The two files** — §1.5 shapes. `.tuning-inventory.json` (measured): `_doc[]`, `schemaVersion 1`, `measuredAtSha/measuredBy/date/note`, `trees { p1: ['src'], p2p3: ['src/domain','src/generators'] }`, `tables{ id → MeasuredTable { exported, idiom, keys, line, spanDigest, leaves, dependents } }`, `unregisteredNamed{ file → n }`, `bareDecimals{ file → n }`, `totals`. `.tuning-register.json` (declared): `registerVersion 1`, `signatureVersion 0`, `signatures: []`, `unitVocabulary: ['ticks','weeks','years','fraction01','probability','share','count','people','mouths','score100','points','multiplier','rank','mixed']`, `homes[]` (§1.5), `tables{ id → DeclaredTable { role, status: 'draft', signedAt: null, unit (per key, all-or-none), band ([min,max] containing the live value), note } }`, `roles{ role → { unit, canonical, members[], divergence: null | { reason ∈ distinct-by-design | unit-differs | drift-suspected, odqRow, note } } }` (seed: `prune_epsilon` drift-suspected · `max_lookback_ticks` drift-suspected · `half_life_ladder` null (the routing wave owns the sites) · `interval_weeks` null · `exodus_floor__fraction`/`__headcount` two roles · `score_max__points`/`__probability` · `cap__share`/`cap__count` · `cooldown_ticks` distinct-by-design · `severity_floor` drift-suspected), `moduleSide[]` (⟦A39 O8⟧ paths the map-module descope's STRIP charter deletes — `src/domain/townCartography/**` at minimum: `cartographyTuning.js:193 TOWN_CARTOGRAPHY_TUNING` still exists at `fab576aba`, STRIP-4 unlanded, 11 files — enrolled in the inventory as P1 tables (totality stands) but EXCLUDED from `--desk` and REFUSED `status: 'signed'` by arm 44, because the owner ruled at §725.1 'no map leg in launch tuning at all; every map constant is module-side' and the launch register may index them but never put them on the sitting's desk; STRIP-4's landing checklist gains `TUNING_INVENTORY_REFREEZE (shrink)` so the deletion does not red arm 13 unpriced — JUDGMENT (fold): the roster over 'TUNEREG boards after STRIP-4/6', because the strip has no date and the register should not wait on it — vetoable), `soakBandAliases{ constantId → { registeredLeaf, phantom, realHome, note } }` (16 distinct; 3 phantoms: `WAR_CRISIS_ARCHETYPES_RATE_PRESS`, `SEVERE_FLIGHT_CAP`, `economicAdj_divisor_400` — real homes are bare literals at `populationDynamics.js:306/:327`, `coup.js:134`; `BASE` qualified to `traditionsKernel.js#TRAD_TUNING.BASE`).

> **JUDGMENT (TUNEREG): TWO files (measured vs declared) over one `src/domain/tuning/tuningRegister.js` module because (1) NEVER HAND-EDIT A MEASURED FIELD becomes structural; (2) zero product bytes with no `ENGINE_SHARED_DOMAIN_EXCISIONS` row to reason about; (3) `tests/copy/voiceMechanics.test.js` scans every string literal under `src/domain` and 208 prose `note`s would enter that ratchet; (4) the pen's diff stays ONE file. Vetoable — the alternative is a src module with a zero-importers walker arm.**

**The arms (8 describes, 41 literal titles; every callback context-free; loops INSIDE bodies with `expect(problems, msg).toEqual([])`; every negative `// anchored:`):** A `'tuning register — the detectors are honest'` (1 spans a frozen table and counts depth-1 keys · 2 resolves a shorthand aggregator one hop · 3 the span digest is blind to comments/whitespace and sees a value change · 4 a decimal inside a registered span is not bare · 5 a decimal in a comment/string is not counted, a template interpolation is · 6 an UPPER_SNAKE numeric const is P2 not P3, an aggregator leaf neither · 7 a string containing `//` does not hide the following literal (the URL trap) · 8 dependents follow one re-export hop). B `'— every table is registered'` (9 every measured table has a declared row · 10 no phantom rows · 11 the three colliding names resolve to seven distinct ids · 12 every declared home export exists as a frozen top-level const). C `'— the inventory is frozen and honest'` (13 exact tree match incl. digests/leaves/dependents overlaid with `DECLARED_GROWTH` · 14 totals · 15/16 bare-decimal not above/not below baseline · 17/18 unregistered-named · 19 the six ceilings never rise (`BARE_DECIMAL_CEILING`, `UNREGISTERED_NAMED_CEILING`, `UNITLESS_TABLE_CEILING`, `DECLARED_DIVERGENCE_CEILING`, `PHANTOM_ALIAS_CEILING = 3`, `DECLARED_GROWTH_CEILING`) · 20 non-vacuity: `relationshipRulesCore.js` ≥ 100 bare decimals and `GROWTH_TUNING` seen with 13 keys). D `'— units and bands are finite'` (21 closed vocabulary · 22 units per table all-or-none · 23 every band contains the live value · 24 a unit-less table is not signed · 25 the unit-less count only shrinks). E `'— one value, one home'` (26 every role member resolves and carries the role unit · 27 one value or a typed divergence · 28 the homonym arm (vacuous at car 1, STATED, non-vacuity by fixture) · 29 divergence count only shrinks). F `'— the signature is a record, not a word'` (30 unsigned today: `signatureVersion 0`, every table draft · 31 a signed table's live digest equals the recorded digest · 32 versions dense from 1, dated, § cited, ids signed at that version · 33 no signature names a GOLDEN shift record while the golden register is unfrozen; when frozen the record exists and its `cause` matches `TUNING_SHIFT_CAUSE_RE` with the same version and `odqRow` · ⟦A9 P4⟧⟦CHAIR-R7⟧ **33b THE CONVERSE** — for every `docs/shift-records/*.json` whose `cause` matches `TUNING_SHIFT_CAUSE_RE v(N)`, `signatures[N]` EXISTS, its `odqRow` equals the record's, and `signatures[N].goldenShiftRecord` names that file (both ways, exact); without it a record citing 'tuning-register v2' can be written and consumed while `signatures[]` tops at v1 and the golden moves under a tuning cause the register never signed — the door verifies FORM only (`goldenRecordDoor.js:136-190`: five string fields + verb + `predictedRows` + `proofForm`; it knows nothing of `signatures[]`) · **33c** post-freeze, a re-record whose `cause` does NOT match the RE may not coincide with a moved `spanDigest` of any SIGNED table (arm 31 already reds the digest; 33c names the coupling so the two reds read as one cause) · ⟦A43 O10⟧ **43** a `signatures[N]` entry naming a demographics id (any `homes[]` row of the `DEMOGRAPHIC_TUNING_COVERAGE` family) is refused unless the cited `research-lit` cell's `identity` and `tuningRegister.declaredSha256` cover those ids' `digests` at version N — the receipt the values are signed against must have been measured UNDER those values (a planted receipt measured under v0 digests with the ids retuned at v1 reds; owner row CAP-10 rules the consequence) · ⟦A39 O8⟧ **44** no `moduleSide[]` id carries `status: 'signed'` and none appears on the `--desk` sheet (a plant: a signed `townCartography` row reds) · 34 the signature records agree with the register — `REGISTER_VII_SIGNATURE`, `FAITH_TUNING_SIGNATURE` and `DEMOGRAPHIC_TUNING_SIGNATURE` read `signed:false` while their tables are draft (§1.5 `words`)). G `'— the soak-band manifest resolves'` (35 every `constantId` is a registered leaf or a declared phantom · 36 phantoms only shrink — three today · 37 every resolvable `currentValue` equals the live leaf). H `'— the refreeze ritual refuses'` (38 dirty tree refused, the inventory file alone dirty allowed · 39 growth refused unless `DECLARED_GROWTH` attributes it · 40 a signed digest cannot move without a signing record; wrong version refused; the right record admits exactly the ids it names · 41 blank/"1" provenance refused). **+ arm 42 (CAPACITY's rider, §1.5).** Lighting-tuple growth MEASURED at the landing, never predicted.

**The signature.** `status: 'signed'` + `signedAt: N` is written by the sitting ONLY together with a `signatures[]` entry of `version: N` whose `ids[]` names the table and whose `digests[id]` equals the live `spanDigest`; a signed digest is FROZEN — a retune is version N+1 by construction; record A (§1.9) is the GOLDEN coupling; `goldenShiftRecord` MUST be null before the freeze. The D4/F6c/CAPACITY records stay where they are and keep gating their dials; TUNEREG INDEXES them (`kind: 'signature-record'`) and arm 34 makes record and row two views of one act.

**The refreeze ritual.** `TUNING_INVENTORY_REFREEZE='<seat>' TUNING_INVENTORY_NOTE='<why>' [TUNING_SIGNATURE_RECORD=docs/tuning-signatures/<date>-v<N>.json] npx vitest run tests/lint/tuningRegister.walker.test.js` — REFUSES a dirty tree (the inventory file alone dirty allowed), blank/"1" provenance, growth of `unregisteredNamed`/`bareDecimals` without `DECLARED_GROWTH` rows (`{ file: { sites, introducedAt: <40-hex resolved against the object store>, cause: >60 chars } }`, monotone-down ceiling starting at 0 — the transcendental `DECLARED_OVERRUNS` machinery copied whole incl. the fake-sha control), moving a SIGNED digest without a valid record; writes ALL or NONE; EXITS NON-ZERO BY DESIGN. The signing record is the file `signatures[N]` is derived from — the ONE place the ritual writes into the pen's file (only `signatures[]` + `status/signedAt` of the named ids; PLAUSIBLE until built — arm 40 + a plant naming an id outside the record expects the write refused whole).

> **JUDGMENT (TUNEREG): the in-walker env-var ritual over a `scripts/*.mjs --update` CLI, because the refusal logic must live in ONE place and a documented `UPDATE_*` refreeze with no shrink guard is a recorded hazard (`banking-a-ratchet-row-disarms-the-ratchet`: `voiceMechanics`' bare `writeFileSync` silently banked +1013); the CLI is REPORT-ONLY + the one-off `--scaffold-declared` — vetoable.**

**What TUNEREG does NOT do:** gate any runtime path (no bits to fork); move any value (car 4 folds equal values only); replace `proposedSoakBands.js`/`check-tuning-bands.mjs` (the RATIFIED bands stay the soak's targets) nor `autoTunableRegistry.js` (lane A stays empty; its denylist refuses every TUNEREG row by module path); sign.

## §4 SOAKCHAIN — a long-horizon soak IN the check chain

### §4.0 Thesis
The estate owns every instrument this item needs except three: a CALLER for the typed tripwire registry (nothing in any committed pipeline evaluates `population_collapse`, `negative_stock`, `unbounded_growth`, `memory_watermark` — CONFIRMED), a LIVENESS FLOOR in the watchdog (`whole-world-soak.mjs:885-896` computes `frozenTail` and prints it as a FINDING nothing consumes — a 30-year world whose composite hash stops changing at year 5 passes every assertion), and a REGISTER a later run compares against (SK-5's bands read a `deterministicFirings` field nobody writes). So the mechanism is not a new soak (the one-soak law forbids one) — it is ONE pure invariants module (§1.6) consumed by THREE readers: the watchdog's `check()`, a new DETERMINISTIC tripwire row, and a 100-year in-chain leg on a light hand-authored realm at the PRODUCT grain. Tier 2 is the research leg on a schedule with a lit overlay so the register freezes the 300-year curve the sitting signs against. Zero `src/` bytes; engine and closure untouched by construction; every new test file's bill predicted then measured. Three literals are deliberately left to Car 0's execution (the Tier-1 seed count, `minDistinctTypesPerDecade`, the LIT bound allowance's tightened value), each with its derivation rule fixed.

**Re-verified (CONFIRMED-by-me at both tips):** the COUPLED dock's diff over every soak file touches ONLY the lighting tuple and `vite.config.js`, so every product file this design edits is byte-identical between the build tip and the coupled tip.

### §4.1 The mechanism
**The invariants** — §1.6 (`soakInvariants.mjs`). The fold reads `eventTypeCounts` per year (the typed key `observeBehavioralYear` already produces, `behavioral-observation.mjs:874-882`) and `yearlyHashes`; `distinctTypes` = the UNION of type keys over the decade; `hashMoves` = years whose hash differs from the previous (the first year counts as a move); FULL decades only (partial tails reported as `partialTailYears`). No decade epoch is added to the telemetry emitter (`simMetricRegistry.mjs:55-67` refuses resampling) — receipt-side. Why typed events and hashes, not the stressor rhythm: the freshest receipt (`$SP/D2bR-soak-tip.json`, 3y×4s) reports `stressorCounts = [0,0,0]` while `eventCount` was 375/115/95, `distinctTypes` 54/24/18, three distinct hashes — a stressor floor would convict a visibly living world.

**The three readers:** THE WATCHDOG (`whole-world-soak.mjs`, after the population block `:835-843`: `livenessVerdict(...)`; `notExecutable` below one decade else `check()`; receipt gains `liveness: { rows, failures, executable, reason }` ADDITIVE, schema stays 5; the population check imports `POPULATION_ENVELOPE`; the bytes check the ceiling; the wall-time check gains the sample floor) — the check's literal title, one spelling: **`the world keeps moving: every decade carries distinct typed events and the composite state changes every year`**; THE REGISTRY (row `liveness_floor`, deterministic: ⟦A14 P9⟧ ALWAYS folds live from `behavioral.yearly[].eventTypeCounts` + `yearlyHashes` — the fold is pure and cheap; a present writer-side `receipt.liveness` block is a CROSS-CHECK that must EQUAL the live fold, else the typed finding `liveness_writer_disagrees` names the `receiptSchemaVersion` that wrote it — two computation paths for one verdict would let the same world convict or pass by which soak version wrote the receipt, and a writer-side `[]` would silence the fold; an archived receipt still convicts; reads no clock); TIER 1 (`tests/simulation/centuryLegSoak.test.js`).

**Tier 1 — the century leg.** Horizon 100 years, non-negotiable (`CERTIFICATION_HORIZONS.release.years = 100` "the only horizon that can earn the product certificate"; §141 refuses shortened centuries by name). Grain: the product's (§1.1 THE ADVANCE ENTRY, 52 weekly ticks a year).

> **JUDGMENT (SOAKCHAIN): the product grain over the cacophony soak's monthly kernel grain (~4.3× cheaper) because (a) the kernel never sees `one_month` in product; (b) the two population lanes disagree about interval scaling (the legacy lane scales by `intervalMagnitude`; the demographic engine's bands are per-tick weekly with no interval read — CONFIRMED) so at monthly grain the LIT engine would run four times slower in calendar time and the CAPACITY plateau probe would be distorted; (c) the epoch seam and the worker path both live at the orchestrator — vetoable; the monthly grain is the named fallback if Car 0 forbids the product grain.**

The fixture — hand-authored, receipted, light: four settlements one per tier band (`town` plains, `village` forest, `hamlet` riverside, `thorp` mountain), each carrying `population`, `tier`, `config.{tier, terrainType, tradeRouteAccess}`, `economicState.foodSecurity.{dailyNeed, dailyProduction, deficitPct, surplusPct, importDependency, storageMonths, resilienceScore}` (the cure pin's `place()` shape — what makes `foodCapacityOf` receipted), `powerStructure.{publicLegitimacy, factions×3, conflicts}`, two `npcs`, `activeConditions: []`; trade chain a→b→c→d + one `rival` edge; one seeded `famine` stressor (severity 0.85); rules = `full_simulation.rules` verbatim (SHIPPED) and the same spread with `demographicsEnabled: true` (LIT); `rngSeed` literal; `now` pinned. Seeds `'century-a'`, `'century-b'`, `'century-c'`; the file's budget **360 s** on the dev box; the minimum viable file = `century-a` SHIPPED + `century-a` LIT + a 10-year replay = 2.1 hundred-year runs; `b`/`c` added while under budget. Working estimate 0.8–2.0 s/year (PLAUSIBLE — the generated realm is 14 s/yr; a hand realm is the `worldTickCostEnvelope` class); if one century run > 170 s the minimum does not fit → Car 0 STOPS. Assertions (every title literal, failure lists typed): SHIPPED `century-a` — `finalTick === 5200`; `nonFinitePaths` empty every year (roster via the seed-failures idiom); every settlement `isFinite(p) && (p > 0 || died)`; `max(yearlyBytes) < CEILING × 4`; `livenessVerdict.failures` `toEqual([])`; the population ratio PRINTED, NOT asserted; SHIPPED `century-a` REPLAY years 1–10 element-wise equal; LIT `century-a` — `populationEnvelopeVerdict.passed`, no settlement > 50× its start (the certification invariant's figure), every settlement ≤ **1.5×** its `effectiveBoundOf(...)` bound (a whole-pulse allowance over the kernel-only 1.05×; PLAUSIBLE — Car 0 tightens toward the measured max, never loosens past 1.5); SHIPPED `century-b`/`-c` ONE drive test each (finite · alive · bytes · liveness). Memory REPORTED (peak heap) not asserted in-process (vitest shares a heap — a flake instrument); asserted in Tier 2's register on a solo run.

> **JUDGMENT (SOAKCHAIN): a decade, not the century, for the in-process replay — the full-horizon replay belongs to the watchdog and Tier 2; an in-process replay of the same module instance is the weak form (it catches module-scope cache warmth — the twelve `__tickIndexStats` caches make that worth catching) and a second century doubles the cost — vetoable.**
> **JUDGMENT (SOAKCHAIN): the SHIPPED leg REPORTS the population ratio and does not assert the envelope: at 100 years on the shipped preset the ledger records this red (100y×4s failed 2026-07-31; 300y×12s failed bounded) and the ratchet's known-failure census is 10/10 with no headroom — a Tier-1 arm born red could not be banked; the envelope is asserted where it is TRUE by design — the LIT leg — which is also the CAPACITY hook. ⟦CHAIR-R9⟧ When the LIGHTING WAVE lights the preset (before the freeze — not the tuning pass), the SHIPPED leg IS the LIT leg and the reporting arm is retired in the wave's own commit (the file header names the ONE planned edit); the century leg's LIT arm is one of the three pieces of plateau evidence the capacity lighting cites (§2.5 C4′). Vetoable.**

**Tier 2 — the research leg and the register.** Profiles added to `REALM_SCALE_PROFILES`: `research-lit` (300y × 12s × seed 1, `lighting: { demographicsEnabled: true }`) — THE evidence the sitting signs against ("the re-run must plateau, not explode slower", TUNING-DESK §13); `research-lit-4s` (300y × 4s) — the INTERIM cell that fits a hosted 6-h job, a different register identity, never a substitute. `buildRealmScalePlan` copies `lighting` onto each case; the spawn adds `--lighting k=v,…`.

> **JUDGMENT (SOAKCHAIN): the scheduled cell runs the LIT overlay, not the shipped preset — the shipped 300-year behaviour is a documented finding whose weekly re-measurement informs nothing, while the lit engine's 300-year curve has NEVER been executed and is the sitting's input; the overlay lights nothing in product; the register records `identity.lighting` so the figure cannot masquerade. Vetoable.**
> **JUDGMENT (SOAKCHAIN): a SEPARATE workflow file `.github/workflows/soak-research.yml` because every weekly job in `ci.yml` gates on `github.event_name == 'schedule'` alone (a second cron there would fire the mutation sweep, the religion soak, the generation certification and the 30-year watchdog on the research cadence); the new file sits outside `ciCheckParity` by construction and outside the deploy gate; the one `ci.yml`-only pin it escapes (setup-node reads `.nvmrc`) is re-asserted in `soakRegister.test.js`. Vetoable.**

The workflow (`cron: '0 2 * * 6'` Saturdays 02:00 UTC — cadence an OWNER row; `workflow_dispatch` with `profile` input; `runs-on: ${{ vars.SOAK_RESEARCH_RUNNER || 'ubuntu-latest' }}` — a self-hosted label lifts the 6-h cap, OWNER row; `timeout-minutes: 360`; steps: checkout, setup-node `.nvmrc`, `npm ci`, plan `--dry-run`, run `realm-scale-certification.mjs --profile … --output artifacts/soak/research.json` under `--max-old-space-size=6144`, `evaluate-receipt.mjs --aggregate` (`if: always()`), `soak-register.mjs --compare … --propose artifacts/soak/soak-register.proposed.json` (`if: always()`), upload `soak-research-${{ github.sha }}` 90 days). No `package.json` byte. **Cost:** ledger 17.6 h/case at `b66e9551` (26,875 + 35,356 s); at the freshest product-grain datum ~3.5 s/settlement-year: `research-lit-4s` primary+replay ≈ 8,400 s ≈ 2.3 h (fits); `research-lit` ≈ 25,200 s ≈ 7 h before superlinearity (9.1 s/settlement-year measured at 12s) — does not fit a hosted runner, and no lawful trick makes it fit (a checkpoint-chained resume is fix-loop-only with no official verdict; a shortened horizon is refused by name; a smaller seed grid is already one seed). Hence the interim cell, the runner seam, and owner row SOAK-1. The register — §1.6 (compare semantics: `shrink` fails on `>`, `floor` on `<`, `band` on `|Δ| > band × committed`, `exact` on inequality, `ceiling` on `>` evaluated ONLY by the solo job step never the pool (SK-2A), `report` never; any finding exits 1 → the job is red → the chair reads the artifact; CI never writes the register — it writes a PROPOSAL; the chair mints on a clean tree; the first mint is the "first clean run" freeze act; "re-measure, do not inherit" — no LEDGER figure is ever typed in). Shapes: `died` = final 0 with the died flag; `runaway` = final > 50× its value one century earlier; `floored` = final ≤ 0.2× start AND |final − value 50 years earlier| ≤ 2 %; `plateau` = |final − value one century earlier| ≤ 5 % of final and not floored; else `other`; `bifurcated = runawayCount ≥ 1 && flooredCount ≥ 1` ("it bifurcates").

**The two named gaps closed, and one defect found (rider on Car 1):** the registry gains a CALLER (§1.6 `evaluateReceipt` — three wirings: the CLI step on the weekly `world-soak` job and the research job; the pool runner `run.mjs` per completed cell → `rollingReport` + the seed ledger's `firings`; the register CLI); the watchdog gains the liveness floor for EVERY profile (the oracle's tempo/motion checks are `release`-gated — `behavioralContract.js:1031` — and the weekly job runs `weekly`, so today the weekly watchdog passes a frozen world); **the defect**: the soak's wall-time trend check `q4 <= q1 * 8 + 50` (`:870`) has no sample floor — at 3 years `meanOf(ms, 0, 0.25)` averages an EMPTY slice and returns 0, so the row reads `Q1 0.0ms → Q4 11703.0ms` and FAILS; the D2bR probe receipt carries `passed: false, properties: []` for a run whose every world assertion passed (CONFIRMED by parse). Car 1 makes the row NOT-EXECUTABLE below `WALL_TIME_TREND.sampleFloorYears = 4` (§206.2b's third status) with the constant in the one home; no product byte, no receipt of 4+ years changes.

**Data shapes (finite semantics):** `LivenessFailureKind` (3), `DecadeLivenessRow`, `LivenessVerdict.executable` (+ literal `reason`), `SETTLEMENT_SHAPES` (5), `RegisterFigureDirection` (6, asserted against the JSON's `figureDirections`), `RegisterFinding.kind` (6), the `liveness_floor` row's `band` string, the `evaluateReceipt` annotation (the `freezeBlockers` key set, no more). No free string is a fact anywhere in the chain.

## §5 READERREVIEW — the exhaustive review as a READER PROTOCOL aimed at interaction density

### §5.0 Thesis
The exhaustive review is not a code read: it is a READER PROTOCOL over a fixed, deterministic corpus of 30-year campaigns rendered headlessly into the documents a customer actually receives (chronicle, news, chronicler's letter, dossier PDF, World Book, campaign PDF, and the faith/war/power tabs as model dumps), read by a panel of Fable seats against one fixed rubric per system (character · operations · faith · war memory · density · capacity) whose every answer must cite the receipt/address chain so a second reader can check it mechanically, scored on three closed axes (VISIBILITY · LEGIBILITY · COHERENCE), hunted by an adversarial reader over a closed vocabulary of contradiction classes, refuted before it is believed, and discharged into a fix backlog typed by CAR CLASS (WIRING · PROSE · DISPLAY) that the validator refuses to let a lane turn into an engine car. Every byte of the protocol's machinery lands OUTSIDE `src/` (scripts, tests, fixtures, docs), so the engine ceiling and the first-paint closure are untouched by construction and THE PROMISE cannot be moved by the review itself — a finding whose honest cure moves an output goes through the GOLDEN door as a declared shift or to the owner's desk. The protocol's own determinism is a bit claim (a manifest of sha256 hashes a second run must reproduce), its posture is the launch posture MEASURED off the live preset table (never a copied roster), and dark machinery is read on a separately labelled LIT-PREVIEW corpus so "correct but invisible" and "dark by flag" can never be confused (§711.4's discovery-vs-regression law).

**Re-verified by the lane (CONFIRMED-by-lane at `60255ca8e`):** the soak's fixture composer + yearly loop (`whole-world-soak.mjs:194-314, 387-427`); `measureAddressChain` (`behavioral-observation.mjs:751-822`); the Herald subject derivation (`heraldFeed.js:111-137` → `WorldPulseData.js:61-72`); `resolveSubject` (`realmEntityWeb.js:319-336`); `corpus()`/`keyOf`/`hashFor` (`generatorGoldenMaster.test.js:693-746`); the preset catalogue and virtual-key spreads (`simulationRules.js:500-560, 703-800`); the collectors (`generateWorldBook.js:198-261`, `generateCampaignPDF.js:766-797`); the headless dossier render's font re-registration (`fullPdf.render.test.jsx:60-95`); `faithPanelModel` (`temperamentAxis` ABSENT from `patron`, `faithPanelModel.js:182-237`) vs the PDF deity block (`liveWorld.js:200-215`, present); `warMemoryActive` (`concludedWars.js:91-93`) and the public HARD-DENY of `concludedWars` (`worldSnapshotPublic.js:97-108`); `npcInteriority({ npc })` on the dossier card vs the worldState-bearing signature (`npcComponents.jsx:257`, `npcInteriorityRead.js:121`); `soakRules.mjs` exports (`:115, :169, :182`); vitest's config has `exclude` only, NO `include` (`vite.config.js:790-822`); `REALM_SCALE_SOURCE_PATHS` = `scripts/audit` only; `ARM_B_ROOTS` = `src/lib, scripts/telemetry, scripts/soak`; the `tests/helpers/` roster (23 files); the COUPLED dock's landing content (W-OPS voice: `operationsVoice.js` +614, `missionAcceptance.js` +405; L7 `characterReadModel.js` and the `e25c73fbf` town-vs-god disagreement).

### §5.1 The mechanism — four parts, one pipeline
`[A] READER CORPUS` (compose N regions of 4, golden row as member 0; advance 30 y by the soak's loop; render EVERY customer document; write a sha256 MANIFEST a second run must match) → `[B] RUBRIC + VALIDATOR` (fixed questions per system; typed scores, typed citations, closed contradiction classes; the validator REFUSES a citation it cannot resolve in the manifest) → `[C] THE PANEL` (one Fable reader per campaign × system, one ADVERSARY per campaign, one REFUTER per finding — `CONFIRMED | ADJUSTED | REFUTED`, default REFUTED) → `[D] THE BACKLOG` (typed rows: classification, carClass, `outputMoving`, `goldensMoved` MEASURED, `chunk` + `firstPaintDelta` MEASURED; the validator refuses an engine car without an owner row).

**[A] The corpus — the posture is READ, never copied.** `LAUNCH_POSTURE_PRESETS = [DEFAULT_SIMULATION_PRESET_ID, 'dramatic_campaign', 'living_realm', 'full_simulation']` — ⟦A51 L1⟧⟦CHAIR-R4⟧ the DEFAULT preset `realistic_regional` FIRST, read from `simulationRules.js:6` and pinned (`expect(LAUNCH_POSTURE_PRESETS).toContain(DEFAULT_SIMULATION_PRESET_ID)` — a suite arm that fails if the default is absent from the roster): the world the customer is HANDED is the default (every new campaign and instant world starts on it, `worldPlan.js:87-92` `DEFAULT_TONE = 'realistic_regional'`; the compendium calls it 'The default. The region evolves at a measured, realistic pace.'; `living_realm`/`full_simulation` are reachable only through the manual Realm rules dialog), and the volume's first roster read three worlds most customers never choose and never the one they get; the six golden-tier rows render under the DEFAULT preset, the world-alive presets on the fresh regions (the roster JUDGMENT below) — with an EMPTY overlay; ⟦CHAIR-R9⟧ `launchPosture(SIMULATION_RULE_PRESETS, LIGHTING_INVENTORY_LIT_SET) → { ok, lit[], dark[] }` pins that the launch posture EQUALS the LIGHTING-INVENTORY's declared lit set, both ways (after the wave the old pin — 'NO engine-gated VIRTUAL key is `=== true`' — INVERTS: the customer's world is the LIT one; recon fact 4's '29 virtual keys lit in NO preset' is the PRE-wave fact); the manifest's `posture.launch` carries `defaultPresetId` and the lit set's digest. No literal "LAUNCH" flag set exists in code (grep 0); this module is where the launch posture gets a NAME, and the name is a derivation with a pin, not a roster. **`ReaderCorpusRow`** (§1.7): `{ campaignId (stable identity, never an index), posture ∈ 'launch'|'preview', preset ∈ SIMULATION_RULE_PRESETS keys, goldenKey|null (member 0 via §1.2 `configFromGoldenKey`; null = a FRESH region), seed (member i generated at `${seed}-${i}`, the soak's law `:210`), overlay ({} for launch; `PREVIEW_OVERLAY` for preview, applied through `composeSoakRules`), years: 30 }`.

> **JUDGMENT (READERREVIEW): a region of FOUR with the golden row as member 0 + three soak archetypes (city/germanic/river/crossroads · town/germanic/grassland/road · town/celtic/coastal/port · village/norse/mountains/road, `whole-world-soak.mjs:194-199`, skipping the one duplicating member 0's tier), over (a) a region of 525 golden rows or (b) single-settlement campaigns — because the soak's four-member shape is the only region composition the estate has soaked at century scale, a single settlement has no neighbours to trade, war or spread faith with (three of six rubric systems need a neighbour), and 525 members is 40× the measured cost — vetoable.**

`whole-world-soak.mjs`'s `buildFixture` is NOT exported and editing that file moves the certification `sourceFingerprint` and the `soakScriptSeams.test.js` pins (the §4.2 hazard), so `composeReaderRegion(row, { now }) → { campaign, saves }` re-states the composer in `scripts/review/readerCorpus.mjs` (edges/channels/`ensureRegionalGraph({edges, channels}, {now})`/`wizardNews:{currentTick:0, entries:[]}`/spatial canon exactly as `buildFixture :250-314` does; `composeSoakRules({ preset: SIMULATION_RULE_PRESETS[row.preset].rules, seasons: 'preset', overlay: row.overlay })`) and PINS it equal to the soak's on the control campaign (below). **The advance is the soak's loop, seam for seam** (§1.1 THE ADVANCE ENTRY): per year `advanceEpoch = soakAdvanceEpoch({ simulationRules: campaign.worldState.simulationRules, seed, year })`; `result = await simulateCampaignWorldInterval({ campaign, saves, interval: 'one_year', commit: true, now: NOW, autoResolve: true, advanceEpoch, onTickObservation })`; `status === 'paused'` THROWS (the soak's contract-break law `:412`); thread `worldState/regionalGraph/wizardNews`, fold `settlementUpdates` (`:418-427`); `NOW = '2026-07-12T00:00:00.000Z'`. **Per-year capture, because retention lies at year 30** (⟦A62 L12⟧ which is exactly why `tick` is MANDATORY on a citation into `news`/`chronicle-advance`/`letter-*` and `shown_then_retired` exists: a consequence shown in year 3 and retired by year 30 is, to the customer who opens the campaign at year 30, invisible; the validator computes it from the tick against the final feed and FINDINGS.md reports per campaign how many `shown` answers survive at year 30) (`wizardNews.MAX_ENTRIES = 240`, `pulseHistory.MAX_HISTORY = 80` — recon fact 7, CONFIRMED (recon); ⟦G0-66⟧ **MEASURED, AND THE STAKES ARE HIGHER THAN THE DESIGN PRICED: the 240-entry cap is SATURATED AT YEAR 1, not late** — `yearlyNewsRows` measured **240 at every one of the 30 years** while year 1 ALONE minted **429** raw entries, so `MAX_ENTRIES` (`src/domain/region/wizardNews.js:21`) is already truncating in the FIRST year. ⇒ **per-year capture through `onTickObservation` is MANDATORY, not a year-30 nicety (RULED §882.8): a reader who opens the year-30 world and reads `wizardNews.entries` sees at most the last 240 of ~12,900 authored rows — under 2 ⟦A52 L2⟧⟦CHAIR-R5⟧ **SURFACE vs RECORD.** The roster is PARTITIONED: SURFACE documents — `news`, `herald`, `chronicle-advance`, `chronicle-grounding`, `letter-yNN`, `dossier-*.vm`/`.pdf`, `worldbook.*`, `campaign-pdf` — are what a customer sees; RECORD documents — `faith-*`, `war-*`, `power-*`, `npc-*`, `world-y*` — are model dumps, and a locator into one resolves by file read whether or not any tab paints the field (the Faith tab at `60255ca8e` renders only the patron seat, the niche rows, the gated FaithSection and the premium PantheonPanel — `FaithTab.jsx:44-104`; `faithPanelModel.js:209` computes `effects = describeDeityEffects(patronSnap)` into the model and the tab paints NONE of it, its own docblock saying boon and bane 'slot in when those trains land' — so Q-FAI-3 could be scored `shown` with a resolvable `faith-<saveId>` pointer into `effects` for a bane the customer never sees; the same holds for every `warStatus`/`powerStrata` field the WarTab and the power tab do not render). `ReaderCitation.kind` is derived from the document id, and `surface_citation_required` refuses a `shown`/`partial` answer whose only citations are RECORD documents. For the three tabs, JUDGMENT (fold): render the actual React surfaces headlessly — `renderToStaticMarkup(<FaithTab …/>)`, `<WarTab/>`, the power tab — into `faith-<saveId>.tab` / `war-<saveId>.tab` / `power-<saveId>.tab` TEXT documents at a stated entitlement posture (⟦A63 L13⟧), added to the SURFACE set, so a tab fact can be cited as shown; Car 0 gains P0.9 (`renderToStaticMarkup` of the three tabs — ⟦G0-59⟧ through the in-lock `ssrLoadModule` runner with `resolve.alias` mapping `**/store/index.js` to a stub — if it fails, the tabs are declared UNREAD by the protocol and handed to the walk explicitly, never scored `shown` from the dump) — vetoable. ⟦G0-70⟧ **MEASURED: ALL THREE RENDER (TRUE_EXIT 0), so the tabs are NOT handed to the walk and CAN be SURFACE documents** (READERREVIEW Car 0 receipt, `$SP/laneG0RR-receipt.md`) §8 — Faith (`FaithTab.jsx`, default export a `function`) 347 B `<div data-testid="faith-tab" …>`; War (`WarTab.jsx`, `function`) 372 B `<div data-testid="war-tab" …>`; Power (`PowerTab.jsx`, default export an **`object`**) 88 B. ⚠ **RENDERING IS NOT YET A SURFACE: all three produced their HONEST-ABSENCE shell** ("No power structure data.") because the stub carried a settlement but no live `campaign`/`worldState` — **the MECHANISM is proven; a REAL surface document needs the threaded campaign wired into the stubbed store, which is Car 2's work and is NOT proven here.** Three mechanical notes each of which cost the lane a failed run: (1) **`PowerTab`'s default export is an OBJECT** (a `memo`/`forwardRef` wrapper; it also exports the named `PowerTab`) — a detector written as `typeof x === 'function'` MISSES it and reports NO_COMPONENT_EXPORT; test `$$typeof`. (2) **Do NOT put `react` / `react-dom/server` through `ssrLoadModule`** — they are CJS and SSR-external, and routing them through the transformer throws `ERR_AMBIGUOUS_MODULE_SYNTAX`; import them normally so the probe and the tabs share ONE React instance, which is what makes the render valid. (3) The store alias is the whole harness. `dossier-*.vm` ALSO gets a flat text projection beside the JSON (⟦A54 L4⟧ a reader must read prose as prose to judge its register).

> **JUDGMENT (READERREVIEW): the dossier is read as its VIEW MODEL plus rendered BYTES, not as extracted PDF text — no @react-pdf text extractor exists in the repo (the GLYPH arm walks the element tree; `jsPdfPaintedText.js` reads jsPDF streams only), the view model IS what the PDF prints under the parity contract, and the bytes still prove the render (page floors, `%PDF-` magic, byte hash); the visual layer stays the T10 walk's — vetoable. PLAUSIBLE that Car 2 adopts the GLYPH element-tree walk (now the shared `tests/helpers/pdfTextRuns.js`, §1.13) as a text source; Car 0 P0.1 measures.**

**Two corpora, one law: LAUNCH and LIT-PREVIEW.** Recon fact 8, re-read and load-bearing: CHARACTER (paradigm axes/drift/known chart), OPERATIONS (missions/espionage) and WAR MEMORY (`concludedWars`) are DARK to every document surface at `60255ca8e` — `characterDriftEnabled` is not in the virtual roster, `espionageEnabled` is virtual and never `: true`, `envoyDiplomacyEnabled` is explicit `false` in `full_simulation` (`:756`), `warMemoryEnabled` is read at ONE gate and nothing renders the ledger (display-side mention = the public HARD-DENY only). FAITH is partially lit (temperament reaches the PDF deity block, not `faithPanelModel.patron`; the FIELD has no flag). DENSITY is lit (chain and ladder render; rung WORDS do not). CAPACITY is `false` in `full_simulation`, lit in no preset (§2). So a LAUNCH-posture reader at `60255ca8e` would score three systems `invisible` and learn nothing about their surfaces. ⟦CHAIR-R9⟧ **The owner's word changes the premise, not the law:** the LIGHTING WAVE lights every dark mechanism the inventory can light BEFORE the review, so the LAUNCH corpus (rendered after the wave, the walk and the regen) reads the LIT world — `demographicsEnabled`, `espionageEnabled`, `warMemoryEnabled` (once the inventory gives it a lighting home), `neutralNeighborsEnabled` are LIT in the customer's presets, and the review reads them as `shown`/`invisible`, never `dark_by_flag`. The two-corpus law survives for the RESIDUE: the keys the wave could NOT light — a lighting whose cost is a budget raise or a build program is an OWNER ROW (R9(d): the MAT dial's +1,047 B, `irregularForce` without SEAT-7/8, `characterDriftEnabled` if no rules overlay reaches it, the faith FIELD which has no flag) — and the resolving law is the walk's own: "say which greens are DISCOVERY vs REGRESSION when checking dark machinery (§711.4)". Hence TWO corpora, one runner, labelled in every document header and in the manifest: **LAUNCH** (`posture: 'launch'`, overlay `{}`) — the world the customer gets AFTER the wave; a residue system's absence scores `dark_by_flag`, NOT a defect row but the evidence for its R9(d) owner row (FUND it or defer it named). **LIT-PREVIEW** (`posture: 'preview'`, overlay = `PREVIEW_OVERLAY`, §1.1) — the same seeds with the RESIDUE systems force-lit through the soak's own seam; here a system's invisibility IS a surface finding (`correct_but_invisible`). `PREVIEW_OVERLAY` is now DERIVED, not authored: the inventory's dark-residue set at the review's tip (frozen into the manifest with its digest; each key with its gate cited); the volume's first frozen list — `warMemoryEnabled` (`concludedWars.js:91`), `espionageEnabled` (5 files read it `=== true`; W-OPS voice in COUPLED), `demographicsEnabled` (`demographicsRates.js:102-105`), `neutralNeighborsEnabled` (the small-N stasis medicine) — is the PRE-wave overlay, and ⟦G0-61⟧ **Car 0 MEASURED each key's reach, LIT vs DARK on an identical fixture, by two sound detectors** (READERREVIEW Car 0 receipt, `$SP/laneG0RR-receipt.md`) §6:

**The manifest — the corpus's bit claim** (§1.7 `ReaderCorpusManifest`): `runId` is a directory name only, NEVER inside a hashed document; `sourceSha` = `git rev-parse HEAD` of the rendering tree; `tuning` (§1.5 — `'unregistered'` fail-closed until TUNEREG lands; every value DRAFT to a reader unless `state === 'signed'`); `posture { launch: the three presets' rules as READ, preview: the overlay as applied }`; per campaign `yearlyWorldHashes[30]` (the "same seed, same world" bits), `addressChain[]` (per-year `measureAddressChain`: rows, `fullyAddressedRateMilli`, `depthHistogram`), `liveness[]` (= SOAKCHAIN's `DecadeLivenessRow[]` BY IMPORT of `foldDecades`, §1.6/§13 C10), `documents { id → { sha256, bytes, pages?, lines? } }`. `--verify <manifest>` re-renders and exits non-zero on the FIRST differing hash, printing campaign/document/year. Two lanes reading "the same campaign" are provably reading the same bytes.
